veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ  patippakam
eṇ. 2,  ciṅkāravēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉai - 600 017.
to.pē.: 24339030, cel: 9444410654
miṉṉañcal: naṭayaeyaṇāyapayavēa@paayaṭai.uṭia


tāḷ : 16.0 ki. mēplittō,  aḷavu : 1/8 temmi 
 eḻuttu : 11.5 puḷḷi,  pakkam : 368  kaṭṭamaippu : iyalpu  paṭikaḷ : 1000  nūlākkam : kō. cittirā, pa. kayalviḻi 
 aṭṭai vaṭivamaippu : celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ) 
 accu : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ,  
āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉai - 600 006.

nuḻaivurai
tamiḻ marapukaḷait talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyākak kāttu varum tamiḻccamūkaṅkaḷil talaiyāya camūkam nāṭṭuk kōṭṭai nakarattār camūkam. intac camūkam īṉṟeṭutta arumperum aṟiñarum, nāṭu, moḻi, iṉam, camutāyam, kalvi, ilakkiyam, vāḻvu mutalāṉa palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟit tam teḷḷat teḷinta cintaṉaikaḷāl tamiḻ ilakkiya vāṉil cintaṉaic ciṟpiyāka valam vantavar mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār āvār.
ipperuntamiḻ ācāṉ vāḻnta kālattil tamiḻarkku eyppil vaippāka eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuccelvaṅkaḷait tokuttu mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa.māṇikkaṉāriṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivaip pōṟṟum vakaiyil māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ eṉum talaippil 18 tokutikaḷāka tamiḻkūṟum nallulakam payaṉ koḷḷum vakaiyil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.
“mūtaṟiñar māṇikkaṉār tamiḻ ārāycci ulakil tamiḻp pēroḷiyāy viḷaṅkiyavar; avaruṭaiya āyvu neṟi tamiḻ marapu cārntatu. tolkāppiyattiṉ putumaik kūṟukaḷaiyum, pāratiyiṉ paḻamaik kūṟukaḷaiyum kaṇṭu kāṭṭiyavar. uraiyāciriyarkaḷaip paṟṟi nirampac cintitta uraiyāciriyar. tolkāppiyattiṟkum, tirukkuṟaḷukkum teḷivurai kaṇṭavar. marapuvaḻip pulamaiyiṉ kaṭaici vaḷaiyamākat tikaḻntavar, āyvu vaṉmaikkut `tamiḻkkātal’, cintaṉait teḷiviṟku `vaḷḷuvam’, pulamai nalattiṟkuk `kampar’ ākiya ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷait tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ iṉam kāṭṭi makiḻvar. ” eṉṟu maṇivācakar patippakattiṉ niṟuvaṉar ayyā ca. meyyappaṉ avarkaḷ ipperuntamiḻ ācāṉiṉ aṟiviṉ parappai viyantu pēcukiṟār.
vaḷarntu varum tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattiṟku va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār patitta patippuc cuvaṭukaḷ putiya vaḻikāṭṭa vallaṉa. tām eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyai nilaināṭṭiyavar. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷiṉ āyvut tiṟaṉum koḷkai uṟutiyum; tiru.vi.ka.viṉ moḻinaṭaiyum camutāya nōkkum ivar nūlkaḷil mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittokuppukaḷaip payilvōr ivvuṇmaiyai muḻutāy uṇara muṭiyum.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam tamiḻ - tamiḻarkkup perumai cērkkum payaṉuḷḷa nalla tamiḻ nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil taṉittaṉmaiyuḷḷa niṟuvaṉam eṉpatai niṟuvi varukiṟatu. itaṉ perumaikkup perumai cērkkum vakaiyil mūtaṟiñar cemmal va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvataṉ mūlam uvakaiyum, kaḷippum, pūrippum aṭaikiṟēṉ. ivar nūlkaḷai nāṅkaḷ veḷiyiṭuvatai eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭaitta naṟpēṟāka eṇṇi eṇṇi makiḻkiṟēṉ.
nalla tamiḻ nūlkaḷaip patippittut tamiḻkūṟum ulakiṟku aḷikkum pōtellām eṉ cintai makiḻkiṟatu; puttuṇarvum, pūrippum, putteḻucciyum aṭaikiṟēṉ. tamiḻp patippu ulakil uyarvutarum neṟikaḷai mēṟkoḷḷa uṟuti ēṟkiṟēṉ. 
naṉṟikkuriyavarkaḷ
maṇivācakar patippaka niṟuvaṉar ayyā ca. meyyappaṉ avarkaḷ ivvaruntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟai veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum aruntoṇṭu ceytavar. inta nērattil avarai naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ. 
pāri nilaiyam maṟṟum kāraikkuṭi celvi puttaka nilaiyam ākiya iraṇṭum peruntamiḻ aṟiñar va.cupa.mā. nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷataiyum innērattil naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ. 
pēraṟiñariṉ makkaḷ aṉaivarum innūl tokuppukaḷ ellā nilaiyilum ceppamuṭaṉ veḷivaruvataṟku utaviṉar. iccentamiḻt tokuppukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku mūla vicaiyāy amaintavarkaḷ tirumati. teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ, tirumati mātari veḷḷaiyappaṉ ākiya ivviru peṇmakkaḷ  māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ tamiḻ ulakam payaṉ koḷḷum vakaiyil veḷivaruvataṟkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāka iruntatai naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu  niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.
moḻiyai mūccākavum, aṟattai vāḻvākavum, ilakkiya vēḷviyai viṉaiyākavum ākkik  koṇṭavar mūtaṟiñar va.cupa.mā avarkaḷ eṉpatai mēlaiccivapuri, kaṇēcar kalai aṟiviyal kallūri tamiḻ uyarāyvu maiyam iṉam kāṭṭi uḷḷatu. 
itu mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār kūṟiya pukaḻ varikaḷ.
“māṇikkac celva maṇittamiḻ aṉṉaikkuk
  kāṇikkai yāyvāytta kaṇṇāḷa - pēṇikkai
  vaitta tuṟaiyellām vāynta tuṟaiyākki
  vaittanī eṅkaḷiṉ vaippu”
ippāṭal varikaḷukku ēṟpa vāḻntu kāṭṭiya  niṟaitamiḻ aṟiñariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuvataṉ mūlam niṟaivaṭaikiṟēṉ.
“taṉakkeṉa vāḻvatu cāvukku oppākum
      tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻvatē vāḻvatākum”
eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivil koḷvōm.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ


nūlākkattiṟkut tuṇai niṉṟōr
kaṇiṉi ceytōr:
tirumati kō. cittirā	tirumati pa. kayalviḻi
tirumati vi. hēmalatā	tiru. ṭi. irattiraṉarācu
nūl vaṭivamaippu:  tirumati  kō. cittirā
mēlaṭṭai vaṭivamaippu: celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ)
mutal tiruttatiṟku utaviyōr:
tirumati. teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ	tiru. pulavar. ta. āṟumukam tiru. ka. karuppaiyā	muṉaivar. ka. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ	pulavar. mu. irācavēlu	tiru. nāka. cokkaliṅkam
iṟutit tiruttam :	muṉaivar mātari veḷḷaiyappaṉ
nūlākkattiṟku utaviyōr:
irā. paramēcuvaraṉ, ku. marutu, vi. matimāṟaṉ
accaṭippu maṟṟum nūl kaṭṭamaippu:
veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭarcu, āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉai.
`māṇikka viḻumiyaṅkaḷ’ ellā nilaiyilum ceppamuṟa veḷivaruvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyāka irunta ivarkaḷukkum maṟṟum aṟiñar perumakkaḷukkum  em neñcam niṟainta naṉṟiyum pārāṭṭum.
 


aṟimukavurai
cemmal va.cupa.māṇikkaṉār
tamiḻccāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ muṉṉōr eṭuttu vaitta aṭiccuvaṭṭil tāmum kāl patittu eḻuttukkaḷaiyum āyvukaḷaiyum ceyta kālattil taṉakkeṉa oru taṉi vaḻiyai amaittukkoṇṭavar cemmal avarkaḷ. tamiḻakattil piṟantu iraṅkūṉil vaḷarntu vāymaik kuṟikkōḷait taṉ vāḻviṉ kuṟikkōḷāka eṇṇiya paḷḷippaṭippai niṟaivu ceyyāta oru ciṟuvaṉai aṭaiyāḷam kaṇṭāḷ tamiḻaṉṉai. acciṟuvaṉait tamiḻkkoṭaiyāka ulakiṟkut tantāḷ. paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār tuṇai koṇṭu tamiḻaik kacaṭaṟak kaṟṟavar, va.cupa. collum ceyalum oṉṟāka vāḻntavar.
tamiḻkkoṭaiyāka vanta va.cupa. tamiḻukkuc ceyta koṭai mika atikam. tamiḻāl aṭaiyāḷam kāṇappeṟṟa avar kaṟṟōrukku mutaṉmaiyarākavum ellōrukkum talaivarākavum viḷaṅkiṉār. caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷai putiya nōkkil āyvu ceytavar. iṉi varum kālaṅkaḷil caṅka ilakkiyam kaṟpāriṉ nuḻaivāyil va.cupa.viṉ akattiṇai āyvu nūl `tamiḻkkātal’.
irāmāyaṇattil paṟṟillāmal iruntavarkaḷaiyum `kampar’ eṉṟa nūl kamparāmāyaṇattaik kaṟkat tūṇṭukiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattiṉ peyarkkāraṇattai āyvu ceytatu `entac cilampu’. tirukkuṟaḷaip putiya kōṇattil āyvu kaṇṭatu `vaḷḷuvam’ maṟṟum `tirukkuṟaṭcuṭar’. tirukkuṟaḷiṉ mūlamum uraiyum oṭṭippiṟanta iraṭṭaiyar pōl kāṇum `urai naṭaiyil tirukkuṟaḷ’. tolkāppiyattai veḷiyulakiṟkuk koṇṭu vantu aṟiñarkaḷiṭaiyē nilainiṟuttiyavar va.cupa. 
tamiḻkkoṭai maṭṭumaṉṟu oru tamiḻāciriyaṉālum aṟakkoṭai ceyya muṭiyumeṉpatai nilaināṭṭiyavar. aṟam ceyya paṇam vēṇṭiyatillai. maṉam tāṉ vēṇṭum. aḻaippu vēṇṭiyatillai. uḻaipputtāṉ vēṇṭum. aṟivu kūṭa vēṇṭiyatillai. aṉpu kūṭa vēṇṭum. eṉpatai uṇarttiyavar. cemmal va.cupa. avarkaḷiṉ pukaḻ tamiḻ vāḻum kālamellām nilaittu niṟkum.
anniya mōkattil aṭimaippaṭṭu, urimai keṭṭu, āṭciatikāram tolaittu allaluṟṟukkiṭanta intiya maṇṇil, akkiṉik kuñcukaḷai vaḷartteṭutta aṇṇal kāntikkup pārati pāṭiya vāḻttup pā itu:
“vāḻka nī! emmāṉ, inta vaiyattu nāṭṭi lellām
  tāḻvuṟṟu vaṟumai miñci viṭutalai tavaṟik keṭṭup
  pāḻpaṭṭu niṉṟa tāmōr pārata tēcan taṉṉai
 vāḻvikka vanta kānti makātmā! nī vāḻka vāḻka!”
itaip pōlavē, tamiḻ maṇṇil anniya moḻikaḷiṉ ātikkattiṟkut tamiḻar aṭipaṇintu, moḻi urimai iḻantu, tamiḻk kalvikkup peruṅkēṭu nērnta pōtu, inta avalattai māṟṟum muyaṟciyil taṉṉaiyē arppaṇittuk koṇṭavar aṇṇal va.cupa.mā.
aṇṇal kāntiyai pārati pāṭiyatupōl, aṇṇal māṇikkaṉārai,
“vāḻka nī emmāṉ, iṅku vantēṟi moḻimō kattāl
  tāḻvuṟṟu urimai keṭṭut tamiḻkkalvi taṉaiyum viṭṭup
  pāḻpaṭṭup parita vitta paintamiḻ taṉṉaik kāttu
  vāḻvikka vanta putiya vaḷḷuvamē vāḻka! vāḻka!
eṉṟu pāṭip paravat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
- teṉṟal aḻakappaṉ
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)



māṇikkam - tamiḻ vārppu
vāymai vāḻvait taṉ uyirākavum, tiruvācakam, tirukkuṟaḷ, tolkāppiyam mūṉṟiṉaiyum pōṟṟip pātukāttu poyyāmai, tūymai, nērmai, eḷimai eṉṟa nāṉku tūṇkaḷaiyum aṭittaḷamākavum varalāṟākavum koṇṭu vāḻntavar tamiḻc cemmal, mūtaṟiñar mutu pērāyvāḷar, peruntamiḻk kāvalar, mutuperum pulavar, tolkāppiyat takaiñar eṉatu tantai va.cupa. māṇikkaṉār.
peṟṟōrkaḷ illāmalē vaḷarntālum taṉṉai naṟpaṇpukaḷālum nalloḻukkattālum naṟcintaṉaikaḷālum cetukkik koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ maṟaiviṟkup piṉ tāyār tirumati. ēkammai māṇikkam avarkaḷ tantai iṭṭa puḷḷikaḷaik kōlamākkiṉārkaḷ. appā taṉatu vāḻnāḷil 50 āṇṭukaṭku mēlāka tamiḻaip pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ. tāyār (tantaiyiṉ maṟaiviṟkup piṟaku) 20 āṇṭukaṭku mēlāka tantaiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷkaḷaip pōṟṟi niṟaivēṟṟiṉārkaḷ. tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷum ārvalarkaḷum iṉṟu tantaiyaip pōṟṟukiṟārkaḷ.
cemmaliṉ aṟivuṭaimai tamiḻaka aracāl potu vuṭaimai ākkappaṭṭatu. eṉ tantaiyiṉ toṇṭukaḷ palatiṟattaṉa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāviṉ nilaippayaṉāka cemmaliṉ nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum maṟupaṭiyum patippittu avaṟṟai tamiḻulakiṟkuk kiṭaikkumpaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum eṉavum cemmaliṉ paṭaippukkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatu viḻāviṟku uyarvum nūlkaḷ vaḻivaḻi kiṭaikkavum tēṭaliṉṟik  kiṭaikkavum vaḻi vakukka vēṇṭum eṉṟum makkaḷ nāṅkaḷ eṇṇiṉōm.
nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāp paṇiyāka  māṇikkaṉār nūlkaḷai yellām veḷiyiṭum amaiyattu cemmaliṉ tamiḻmaiyai tiṟaṉāyntu tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷiṉ muttiraikaḷum nūlkaḷāka  veḷivarukiṟatu. ivvāṇṭil poṟuppeṭuttu veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippakattiṟkum em tantaiyiṉ tamiḻppaṇik kuṟittu perumai tarum nūlkaḷāka eḻutiyuḷḷa  nūlāciriyar kaṭkum tamiḻiṉamē naṉṟi kūṟum.
muṉaivar. mātari veḷḷaiyappaṉ
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)
kōvai	
11.8.2017
eṉ aṉpuṟai
eṭṭu vayatu pālakaṉāka iruntapōtē īṉṟeṭutta peṟṟōrkaḷ viṇṇulaku eytiya cūḻnilai.
iḷam vayatilēyē “lēvātēvi” eṉṟu collappaṭum vaṭṭit toḻilaik kaṟṟuk koḷḷa parmā ceṉṟu vēlaippārtta iṭattil mutalāḷi, varumāṉavari atikāriyiṭam poy collumpaṭi appāvai vaṟpuṟuttiyatāl, tiṇṇamāka maṟuttuviṭṭut tāyakam tirumpiya uttama maṉitar.
aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakattil paṇṭitamaṇi, katirēcaṉāriṭam tamiḻ payiṉṟu, piṟkālattil kāraikkuṭiyil tāṉ kaṭṭiya illattiṟku “katirakam” eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭi, ācāṉukku mariyātai celuttiya māmaṉitar.
ciṟpi oru kallai uḷiyaik koṇṭu cetukkic cetukki, ciṟanta ciṟpamākkuvāṉ. atēpōl, taṉ maṉatai eḷiya vāḻvu, etirkāla nampikkai, teḷivāṉa cintaṉaikaḷ, vāḻkkaikkāṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ, uyarnta kuṟikkōḷ, uṉṉata māṉa ceyal, vāymai, tūymai, nērmai, iṟai vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟa eṇṇat tiṇmaiyuṭaṉ taṉṉaittāṉē pakkuvap paṭuttik koṇṭatāl, uyar pataviyāṉa tuṇaivēntar patavi appāvai nāṭi vantatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam, tolkāppiyam ākiya mūṉṟu tamiḻ maṟaikaḷum, paṇpuṭaṉ vāḻvataṟkuriya vaḻikāṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu niṉaittu vāḻvil kaṭaippiṭittu veṟṟi kaṇṭa uyarnta maṉitar.
appā eḻutiyuḷḷa `taṟcintaṉai’ eṉṉum kuṟippēṭṭil “nāṉ paṭippilum eḻuttilum āyvilum patavip paṇiyilum muḻu nēramum īṭupaṭumāṟu kuṭumpap poṟuppai muḻutum tāṅkiyavaḷ ēkammai eṉṟa eṉ orē maṉaivi” eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷatiliruntu appāviṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku ammā muḻu uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatai nāṉ kuṟippiṭa virumpukiṟēṉ.
cumār aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kalvikku mika mukkiyattuvam koṭukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ tāytantaiyar iruvarukkum orumittak karuttāka iruntatāl, taṉ kuḻantaikaḷ aṟuvaraiyum paḷḷip paṭippōṭu niṟuttiviṭāmal, āṇ, peṇ eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu illāmal paṭṭap paṭippukkaḷ varai payila vaittu veṟṟi kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
eṉ iḷaṅkalai vaṇikaviyal cāṉṟitaḻilum, eṉ kaṇavariṉ poṟiyiyal cāṉṟitaḻilum tuṇaivēntariṉ kaiyoppa iṭattil eṉ tantaiyāriṉ kaiyoppam irukkum. itai nāṉ peṟṟa pākkiyamāka niṉaikkiṟēṉ.
nāṉ peṅkaḷūril iruntapōtu, appā eḻutiya ovvoru kaṭitattilum, “avinayaṉ tirukkuṟaḷ collu kiṟāṉā? putitākac collum kuṟaḷukku ayyā kācu taruvārkaḷ eṉṟu col” eṉṟa varikaḷ niccayamāka irukkum. eṉ makaṉ iḷam vayatilēyē tirukkuṟaḷ kaṟka vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ tantaiyāriṉ eṇṇam.
iḷañciṟārkaḷukkup piñcu neñcaṅkaḷilēyē tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam paṭittal, iṟaivaḻipāṭu ceytal pōṉṟa vitaikaḷai vitaittu viṭṭāl, muḻuvāḻvum mikavum ciṟappāka amaiyum eṉpatu tantaiyāriṉ tiṇmaiyāṉa karuttu.
mikac ciṟu vayatil peṟṟōrai iḻanta cūḻnilaiyil, aṭippaṭai poruḷātāram illāta nilaiyil, atikamāṉa uḻaippālum, muyaṟciyālum, nērmaiyāṉa vaḻiyil tāṉ īṭṭiya poruḷātārattil, atāvatu taṉ cottil āṟil oru paṅkai taṉatu conta ūrāṉa mēlaiccivapuriyiṉ mēmpāṭṭukkāka uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai pākupāṭuyiṉṟi celavu ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu taṉ viruppamuṟiyil eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tantaiyāriṉ viruppappaṭiyē tāyār avarkaḷ 20-4-1992 il appāviṉ peyaril aṟakkaṭṭaḷai ārampittu naṭatti vantārkaḷ. iṉṟum toṭarntu naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṟatu.
appā eḻutiya muppatukkum mēlāṉa nūlkaḷil `vaḷḷuvam’, `tamiḻkkātal’, `kampar’, `cintaṉaikkaḷaṅkaḷ’ pōṉṟavai tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟil mikacciṟanta āyvu nūlkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.
uṉṉata vāḻkkaikku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka vāḻntuḷḷa eṉ tantaiyāriṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟaip paṟṟi terintu koḷpavarkaḷiṉ vāḻvilum nalla tiruppumuṉai ēṟpaṭṭu vāḻvu cemmaiyākum eṉpatu uṟuti.
poṟṟoṭi centil
(va.cupa. māṇikkaṉāriṉ makaḷ)
mumpai	
21-7-2017.






nakatarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ
mutaṟ patippu 1963
innūl 1969il  pārinilaiyam veḷiyiṭṭa patippai mūlamākak koṇṭu veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.


patippurai
nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ eṉṉum innūlil eṭṭuvakai ēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.pi.1600 mutal 1800 varai eṉṟu matippiṭalām. nāṭṭuvaralāṟu eṉpatu pōrc ceyalkaḷaiyum aracarkaḷiṉ ceytikaḷaiyum maṭṭum kuṟippataṉṟu; kuṭimakkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷaiyum camutāya naṭappuk kaḷaiyum kūṟavēṇṭum. avvakkālattu maṉappāṉmaikaḷaik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. ivvēṭukaḷ paḻaṉittalattu munnūṟṟaimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka nikaḻntuvarum camaya naṭappukkaḷaiyum naṭattiyavarkaḷaiyum avarkaḷiṉ aṟputac ceyalkaḷaiyum acceyalkaḷai viyanta aracarkaḷaiyum avvaracarkaḷiṉ camaya matippiṉaiyum virivākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivvaṟaṅkaḷaic ceytaruḷiyavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ; ātaliṉ nakarattār varalāṟṟiṉaiyum perumaiyiṉaiyum ivvēṭukaḷ cuṭaṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa pakkam koṇṭa ivvēṭukaḷ pāṇṭiyar, cōḻar, maturai nāyakkar, civakaṅkaiccīmai marutu, putukkōṭṭait toṇṭaimāṉ mutaliyōraip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷaik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ varalāṟṟāciriyar kaḷukkut tuṇai ceyyum eṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ. munnūṟṟaimpatu āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ pēccut tamiḻppaṭi eḻutappaṭṭa ivvēṭukaḷ tamiḻmoḻi yārāycciyāḷarkkum nalla tuṇai ceyyum. camayam paṟṟiya pala marapukaḷ iruttaliṉ, caiva camaya varalāṟṟiṟkum īṇṭuccāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. ivvaccu nūlil aṭaṅkiya ēṭukaḷiṉ vakai:-
paṭṭayattiṉ amaippu muṟaikaḷ
cāli, kali, tamiḻ eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakai āṇṭukaḷum tiṅkaḷ nāḷ kiḻamai ōraikaḷum pala paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉār muṉpāka iṉṉār koṭutta tarmacācaṉappaṭṭayam eṉṟu eḻutumuṟaiyaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. viḷakkamākavum muṭivil tokuttum cācaṉa nōkkam tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. camayattuṟaiyil yār yārkku eṉṉeṉṉa mariyātaikaḷ, mariyātai varicaikaḷ avvaricaikkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ, aṟputa nikaḻccikaḷ, pūcai naṭappukkaḷ, annaṭappukku vēṇṭum poruḷ taraṅkaḷ ellām aiyattiṟkum vaḻuviṟkum iṭamiṉṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaikkuṟikkum nōkkamē ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya nōkkamākum. oru paḻaṅ kaiyeḻuttup puttakappaṭiyiliruntu innūl accākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum mūlappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ ēṭukaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ovvoru paṭṭayattiṟkum ettaṉai ēṭukaḷ eṉpataṉai mēl aṭṭavaṇaiyiṟ kāṇalām. ivvaṟap paṇiyai ulakam uḷḷavarai naṭattuvōm eṉavum, etu nilai tirintālum ivvaṟanilai tiriyōm eṉavum, ivvaṟattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu ceytavarkaḷ iṉṉapaḻikku āḷāvārkaḷ eṉavum, utavi ceytavarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻvārkaḷ eṉavum iṟutiyil viṭātu kuṟippiṭuvatu ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkāṟākum. iccācaṉam eḻutiṉatu iṉṉār coṟpaṭikku iṉṉāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭi muṭikkappaṭum.  yār eḻutiṉāl cācaṉa nōkkam valuppaṭumō avaraikkoṇṭu eḻutaccollum aṟivuṭaimaiyaip pārkkiṉṟōm. paṭṭayattiṉ toṭakkattilum kaṭaiciyilum murukaṉ pāṭalkaḷ iruttaluṇṭu. iṉi ovvoru paṭṭayattiṉ karuttiṉaiyum curukkamākak kāṇpōm.
1. paḻaṉikkōyil naṭappu aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 1- 27)
nēmaṅkōyil iḷanala vakuppu kuppāpiccañ ceṭṭi makaṉ kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalañ ceṉṟu uppu vaṇikañ ceytatu, paḻaṉikkōyil teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉaiyil taṅkip paṇṭārattiṉ utaviyaiyum avarmaṉaivi, pārvatiyammāḷ utaviyaiyum peṟṟatu. vēlāyuta cuvāmikku ilāpattil makamaip paṇam cērttatu, piṟakōyil nakarac ceṭṭiyārkaḷum kumarappaṉōṭu uppu vāṇikattiṟkuc cērntu koṇṭatu. uppuc ceṭṭikaḷ eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. (kalāmaṭam)īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi kumarappaṉ paḻaṉikkuk kāvaṭi kaṭṭiyatu, iṉṉiṉṉa iṭattil taṅkic ceṉṟu,aintu nāḷ makēcuvara pūcai ceyvatu, ēḻu kōvil nakarattārum kāvaṭi eṭuttatu. īcāṉiya kurutēcikar kumarappaṉ mutalāṉavar paciyaip pōkkiyatu, pūcaikkup piramputtaṭi aruḷiyatu, kumarappaṉ campaṭṭiyil nattaliṅka nāyakkar maṉaiviyiṉ vayiṟṟuvaliyaip paḻaṉiyappar aruḷāṟ pōkkiyatu. nāyakkar maṉaivi kumarappaṉ vīṭu tēṭivantu naṉṟi celuttiyatu, nāyakkar iraṇṭu vēlāyutam paḻaṉiyapparukkuk koṭuttatu, vēlāyuta cuvāmi pārvatiyammāḷukkum kumarappaṉ peṇcāti ciṭṭāḷukkum īcāṉiyacivācāri yārukkum āyikkuṭi camīṉtārukkum kaṉavil tōṉṟic colliyatu, camīṉtāriṉ curanōyaip pōkkiyatu, maṇṭakappaṭi naṭattuvatu, paṇṭārattu maṉaiyai maṭamāka vaittuk koḷvatu, paṇṭārattukkuk kuṭumpattārkkum vaḻi vaḻi naṉṟi maṟavātiruppatu eṉṟa karuttukkaḷum iṉṉārukku iṉṉa koṭuppatu eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷai varaṉmuṟaikaḷum ivvaṭṭavaṇaiyil collappaṭṭuḷa.
maṇṭakappaṭiyil paṇṭārattukku aritip parivaṭṭamum, kumarappaṉukku alaṅkārap parivaṭṭamum, nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tirunīṟum kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉavum, nakarappotuviliruntu (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarukkum (pātarakkuṭi) tiruppuṉal tēcikarukkum, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum, kumarappaṉukkum koṭuppatu eṉavum, iṉam oṉṟukkut tirunīṟṟuppai oṉṟu koṭuppatu eṉavum mariyātaikaḷ varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (pak.24)
2. ēḻunakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 28 -42)
itu teyvacikāmaṇi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum eḻutikkoṭutta tarumacācaṉam. cāli āṇṭu 1780 eṉpatu piḻaiyeṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. kaliyāṇṭōṭum piṟacācaṉaṅkaḷōṭum ottiṭṭup pārkkumpōtu, cāli 1520 eṉa iruttal vēṇṭum pōlum. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, nakarattār perumaikaḷ, ēḻu nakarattāriṉ irupattiraṇṭu vakuppukaḷ, teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāviṟkum koṭukkavēṇṭum arici mutalāṉa poruḷaḷavukaḷ, tīrttamum tirunīṟum vaḻaṅkum mariyātai varicaikaḷ, pattāntirunāḷ maṇṭakappaṭiyil koṭukkum poruḷaḷavukaḷ, taṅki varavēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ ellām iccācaṉattu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cōḻavaracaṉ nakarattār peṇṇaimaruvip poṟukka muṭiyāta tontaravu ceytapaṭiyāl, iḷaiyāttaṅkuṭi vanta ceytiyaiyum, araca taṇṭaṉaikku okkūruṭaiyāṉ maṟutalai koṭuttapaṭiyāl nakarakkōyilkaḷil okkūruṭaiyāṉukku mutal mariyātai naṭakkiṉṟa ceytiyaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum, nēmam kumarappaṉ paḻaṉittalattukku uppu viṟkavantu paṭṭapirayācaiyai matittu, paḻaṉittalattil mutal vipūtiyum kāḷāñciyum parivaṭṭamum kumarappaṉukku naṭakkaṭṭum eṉṟu (pak.38) eḻu nakarattār aṉaivōrum orēmaṉatāyppēci muṭittaṉar eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa karuttākum. iccācaṉam nakarattār coṟpaṭikku okkūruṭaiyāṉ uṭaiyappa ceṭṭi aṭaikkappaṉ eḻutiyatu. okkūruṭaiyāṉ taṉakku uriya mutal mariyātaiyaip paḻaṉittalattil nēmam kumarappaṉukku viṭṭukkoṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, iccācaṉattai okkūruṭaiyāṉaik koṇṭu eḻutac colliṉar nakarattār eṉa aṟiyavēṇṭum. ēḻu nakarattārum taṉittaṉi āṟu kāl kūṭattil kumarappaṉaik kumpiṭṭu viḻuntu tirunīṟu vāṅkikkoḷvatu eṉṟa mariyātaic ceytiyum, kumarappaṉ cantatiyil mutalvaruṭam paṭṭam kaṭṭukiṉṟa vivaramum aṟiyalām.
3. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 42 - 65.)
itu cāli 1688 - kali 4868 (ki.pi.1766) ām āṇṭil kuḻantai paṇṭārattu makaṉ kumāracāmi paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum aḷitta cācaṉappaṭṭayam. mēlaiccācaṉam pōla yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum nakarattār perumaikaḷum pirivukaḷum paṇṭārattukkum kuṭumpa vaḻiyiṉarkkum koṭukkum kaṭṭaḷaikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉ vaikāvūr nāṭṭu vicayakiri vēlāyutat turaiyarkaḷukku āṇ kuḻantai piṟakkum eṉṟu colliyatu, turaiyiṉ paṭṭattammāḷ kuṭṭayaṉiṭam makappēṟu kēṭṭatu, kuṉṉakkuṭiyil periyamarutu pāṇṭiyar mūṉṟu kēḷvi kēṭṭut takka viṭaipeṟṟatu, kuṭṭayaṉukkuk kaṇaiyāḻiyum yāṉaikkompum koṭuttatu eṉṟa aṟputaṅkaḷai aṟiyalām.
vicayakirit turaiyum marutupāṇṭiyarum uttara viṭṭapaṭi, iraṇikkōyil pe. kuṭṭayaṉukku aruḷāṭum kāraṇattāl, iraṇṭāvatu kāḷāñciyum mariyātaik kāḷāñciyum koṭuppatu eṉṟa muṭivu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum. mariyātai varicaikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum muṟaikaḷum 59 - 62ām pakkam varai virivāka iṭam peṟṟuḷa. ippaṭṭayattil vicayanakarattu aracarkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ vaḻiyāṇṭa nāyakkar kaḷaiyum makāmaṇṭalēcuvaraṉ eḻupattēḻu pāḷaiyam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum paṟṟic ciṟanta kuṟippukkaḷ uḷa (pak. 47 - 49)
4. ēḻu nakarattār tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 66 -78)
itu cāli 1710 kali 4889 (ki.pi. 1788) ām āṇṭil kumāracāmi paṇṭārattu makaṉ teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattukku ēḻu nakarattārum koṭutta taruma cācaṉappaṭṭayam. nakarattārkaḷiṉ perumaikaḷ pirivukaḷ aintu nāḷ pūcaikkum aṉṉatāṉa maṭattut tiruviḻāvukkum koṭukkum arici poṉ paḻam mutaliyavai, paḷḷayattukkuk koṭukkum poruḷ aḷavukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. veṟṟilai pākku paḻam kāykaṟi kaḷōṭu pōyilaiyum curuṭṭum koṭuppatuṇṭu eṉṟa oru putucceytiyai ippaṭṭayattil kaṟkiṉṟōm. nēmaṅkōvil kumarappaṉukkum iraṇikkōyil kuṭṭayaṉukkum piṟarukkum uriya mariyātai varicaikaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum 73 -76 ām pakkaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷa. teyvanāyaka paṇṭārattu maṉaiyil muṉpirunta āṟukāl kūraik kūṭattaik kaltiruppaṇi ceytu āṟukāl cavukkaiyākkiyatum, 78 kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapam ceytatum kuṟikkattakka muṉṉēṟṟaṅ kaḷām. iṅṅaṉam nakarattār celavu ceytu paṇi perukki ṉālum aṉupavikkum urimai paṇṭārattu vaḻiyiṉarkku eṉṟum uṇṭu. vayiravaṉ kōyil vēlappa ceṭṭi vayiravaṉ tiruppaṇi vēlaiyil mikkamuyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭataṉālum, cuvāmi vayiravaṉiṭam nēril tōṉṟip pēciyataṉālum (pak.76) nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟāvatu mariyātai vayiravaṉukku uriyatu eṉpatu iccācaṉattiṉ uyirāṉa ceytiyākum.
5. piṟāmalai ātīṉam naṭappu aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 79 -85)
paḻaṉikkōyil iṭumpaṉmalai mutal ūr varum varaiyil īcāṉiya civācāriyār, paṇṭāram, kumarappaṉ nakarattār ceyyavēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. iṉṉārukku iṉṉa kaṭamai eṉṟu caṉṉitāṉam colliyapōtu ātīṉa mariyātai taṉakkum vēṇṭum eṉṟu iraṇikkōyil tiruvēṭpū ruṭaiyāṉ kuṭṭayaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷaik kalantālōcittu, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭiyil naṭakkiṟa paṭṭuparivaṭṭamum antac celavum kuṭṭayaṉ taṉatāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṟu cuvāmi colliyatu. caṉṉitāṉam ūrukku varumpōtu kuṭṭayaṉukku mūṉṟāvatu paṭṭup pītāmparam koṭukkiṉṟatu.
6. ātīṉam paṭṭāpiṭēka aṭṭavaṇai (pakkam 86 -90)
kumarappaṉ paḻaṉiyāṇṭavar aṉukkirakam peṟṟa paṭiyāṉum, ātīṉattukku vēṇṭum kāriyaṅkaḷum uḻaippum ceytapaṭiyālum ātīṉappaṭṭāpiṭēkattu. iraṇṭāvatākak kumarappaṉ paṭṭu cāttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu īcāṉakurutēcikar moḻintār. itaṉai irupattoru kōttirattu nakarattār kaḷum cariyeṉa ottukkoṇṭaṉar. vaḻakkampōl naṭakkavēṇumēyoḻiyap putuvaḻakkam ceyyappaṭātu eṉṟu iḷaiyāttāṅkuṭi paṭṭaṇacuvāmi vakuppu aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi aṇṇāmalai maṭṭum caṉṉitāṉattiṉ tiruvaṭiyil viḻuntu maṟuppuk kūṟiṉār. kumarappaṉ perumaiyaik kurutēcikar eṭutturaittapiṉ aṇṇāmalaiyum ottuk koṇṭār. ottukkoṇṭataṟku uṟutiyāka aṇṇā malaiyaiyē aṭṭavaṇai eḻutumpaṭi īcāṉiya civācāriyār kaṇittār.

7. maṭavālayat tarumacācaṉam (pakkam 91 - 100)
itu cāli 1727 kali 4906 (ki.pi.1805) āṇṭil nākappa paṇṭārattu makaṉ kuḻantaivēlup paṇṭārattukku ēḻunakarattār aṉaivōrum koṭutta cācaṉappaṭṭaya mākum. muṉṉaiccācaṉaṅkaḷ pōlap paṇṭārattukkuk koṭukkum naṭappukkaḷum piṟavum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷa. iṭam pōtāmaiyiṉāl aṭuttamaṉai mōḷaiyattēvaṉ vakaiyaṟā iṭattai vilaikku vāṅki ākkupuraiyum muppattoṉpatu kāl tiruppaṇi maṇṭapamum kaṭṭiya vaḷarcci kuṟippiṭattakum. maṭattiṟkāka vāṅkiya immaṉaikaḷum paṇṭārattiṉ anupōkattiṟku uriyaṉavē piḷḷaiyārkōyil vakuppu muttappaṉukkuc civappaṭṭiyil paḻaṉiyāṇṭavaruṭaiya aruḷvanta ceytiyum, toṇṭaimāṉ aracar muttappaṉ vīṭṭiṟku vantu niṉaitta kāriyattaik kēṭṭu makiḻntu cālvai mutaliyaṉa koṭutta ceytiyum, muttappaṉ kēṭṭuk koṇṭapaṭi kōvil vīṭṭukkut tāmpūravēl koṭutta ceytiyum, nēmaṅkōvil paḻaṉiyappaṉ paḻaṉikkōvilukkuk kāvaṭi eṭuttu varumpōtu inta vēlāyutattaiyum vaittuppūcai ceytuvarumpaṭi colliya ceytiyum, itaṉaic colvataṟkākap paḻaṉiyappaṉai araṇmaṉaikku aḻaittuvantu mariyātai ceyta ceytiyum ippaṭṭayattukku mukkiya ceytikaḷākum. (pak. 96 -98) tēvakōṭṭai rattiṉavēlāyutamum kuṉṟakkuṭi vēlāyutamum cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
8. nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar paṭṭayam (pakkam 100 -116)
itu cāli 1549 kali 4730 (ki.pi.1627) ām āṇṭil ēḻu nakarattār muṉpākak kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam nirampa vaḻaṅkiya tēcikar (tuḷāvūr maṭam) koṭutta paṭṭayam. yukaṅkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷum nāyakkar peyarkaḷum muṉṉark kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēcikar perumai, nakarattār perumai, kōttiram, kulacēkarapura cavuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ nakarattārkaḷai naṟkuṭiyākat taṉ cīmaikku aḻaittatu, nakarattāriṉ veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ cōḻaṉ uttira viṭṭum (kalāmaṭam) īcāṉiya kurutēcikarai toḻa maṟuttatu eṅkaḷ nirampavaḻakiya kurutēcikar (tuḷāmaṭam) pāṇṭināṭṭiṟku vantāl nāṅkaḷum pōvōm eṉṟu uraittatu, veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuruviṭam muṟaiyiṭṭatu, cōḻaṉ kurutēcikarai varavaḻaittup pāṇṭiyaṉ pala kirāmaṅkaḷ taruvāṉ eṉṟu moḻintu irupattiraṇṭu kōttirattu nakarattār āṇkaḷum veḷḷāṭṭip peṇkaḷum pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vantatu, pāṇṭiyaṉ ellōraiyum etir koṇṭaḻaittu mākāṇaṅkaḷ carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittatu. kuṉṟakkuṭi ātīṉam amaintatu mutalāṉa nakarattār varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷai ippaṭṭayattut teḷivāka aṟiyalām.
nēmaṅkōyil kumarappaṉum avaṉ maṉaivi veḷḷāṭṭi ciṭṭāḷum uppumāṟi eṭutta makamaiyāl inta ātīṉamum piṟa tarumaṅkaḷum naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉavum, paḻaṉittalattilpōla intattalattilum nakarattārkaḷil mutalāvatu tīrttam tirunīṟu kumarappaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṉavum nirampavaḻakiya tēcikar uttaravuppaṭi nakarattār aṉaivōrum uṭaṉpaṭṭa ceytiyai ippaṭṭayam mukkiyamākac collukiṉṟatu. mariyātai varicaikaḷ 114 -15 ām pakkaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum naṭaimuṟaikaḷum
paṭṭayaṅkaḷil kuṟittuḷ naṭappukkaḷ iṉṟum perumpālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālappōkkil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. ellāp paṭṭayaṅkaḷilum mutal mariyātaikkuriyavarāṉa nēmam kumarappaṉ vaḻi vantōrē iṉṟu neṟkuppai ku.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉarāvar. iraṇṭām mariyātaikku uriya aruḷāṭi iraṇikkōyil pe.kuṭṭayaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kaṇṭaṉūr kupa.cu. vīṭṭiṉar nakarattārkaḷil mūṉṟām mariyātaikku uriya vayiravaṉkōyil vayiravaṉ vaḻivantōrē iṉṟu kāraikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu.paḻa. vīṭṭiṉar. tāmpūravēl peṟṟa piḷḷaiyōr kōvil muttappaṉ vaḻivantavarē iṉṟu mēlaiccivapuri āṟu.muru. kuṭumpattiṉar iṅṅaṉam teyva aruḷpeṟṟa nakarattār nālvar vaḻivantōr ālpōl taḻaittu vāḻntu varutaliṉ, muṉṉōr paṇikaḷ iṭaiyaṟātu kākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uṇartal vēṇṭum. ikkuṭumpattārkaḷ tam kuḻantaikaḷukku murukaṉ peyarkaḷaiyē vaippar eṉpatum niṉaiyattakum.
paṭṭayaṅkaḷum tamiḻum
ēṟakkuṟaiya munnūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉtōṉṟiya iccācaṉat tamiḻ palavakaiyālum ūṉṟi ārāyvataṟku uriyatu eṉṟu maṭṭum īṇṭuc cuṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ urainaṭai varalāṟṟil iccācaṉa naṭai iṭampeṟat takuvatu. oru perum puttaka aḷaviṟku oppāṉa iccācaṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ pēcuvatu pōṉṟa tamiḻ naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oṇṇu, reṇṭu, mūṇu, paṉireṇṭu, aruci, kuṭuttu, rācā, pōyi, tuṇṇuttuppai, matiyāri, ciṟaicākkiṉai, vīṭṭukkup pōratu, pokkucam, naccēttiram eṉappala marūuccoṟkaḷai nirampakkāṇalām. akkālattut tamiḻccoṟkaḷai eṅṅaṉam olittārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷalām. māṟi (viṟṟu) atumēraikku (atupōla) paraṅkaḷ (paratēcikaḷ) vaḷamai (vaḻakkam) peṇṇāṭṭi (peṇṭāṭṭi) neṭṭeḻuttu (kaiyeḻuttu) eṉṟa coṟkaḷaiyum, aṭakeṭuttal, aritipparivaṭṭam, alaṅkārap parivaṭṭam, vēṟpaṭi, aruḷāṭi mutalāṉa coṟkaḷaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. uṇṭumā, meḻukivaittuvai. nāṉum varuka, varuvōmākavum eṉṟa toṭar muṭipukaḷaiyum aṟikiṉṟōm.
nakarattārkaḷ oḷavaiyiṉ tamiḻppāṭṭukkup poṟpāṭakam koṭuttārkaḷ eṉavum, varappuyara eṉṟu oḷavai uḻavarkaḷai vāḻttiyatu pōla maṭiccīlai peruka eṉṟu vaṇikañceyyum innakarattārkaḷai vāḻtti varaṅkoṭuttār eṉavum ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ viḷampukiṉṟaṉa. nāyakka maṉṉaṉ muttamiḻ aṇaṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaḷḷuvamaṟaiyuṅ kaṟṟu muppāl moḻiyumpaṭiyē aṟivai aṟintu allavai kaṭintu nallavai nāṭṭik kuṭitaḻaikka āṇṭāṉ eṉavum moḻikiṉṟaṉa. nāṅkaḷ ēḻu nakarattārkaḷ naṭṭakallaiyum iṭṭacūlamum aḻikka māṭṭōm; makāmērukiri cāyntapōtilum, kaṭal ēḻum vaṟṟiṉa pōtilum, kāvēri vaṟaṇṭa pōtilum, cantirāḷ cūriyāḷ teṟku vaṭakkāṉapōtilum nāṅkaḷ nakaram poycolla māṭṭōm eṉavarum uṟutippāṭukaḷ nakarattāriṉ nāṇayattaip paṟaicāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ūḻi peyariṉum tām peyarār cāṉṟāṇmaikku
āḻi yeṉappaṭu vār
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvamaṟaippaṭi vāḻntavarkaḷ nakarattārkaḷ eṉpataṉai inta aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ kāṭṭikkoṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭaiyōr tam poyyāp paṇpiṉaiyum kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṉaiyum pukaḻiṉaiyum oḻuṅkiṉaiyum aṟitaṟku ippaṭṭayanūl ārāycciyāḷar kaippaṭṭupparavum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. nakarattār camutāyam tamiḻpperuñ camutāyattiṉ oru pakutiyātaliṉ, ippaṭṭayaṅkaḷ tamiḻiṉattiṉ nākarika vaḷarcciyaik kāṇa muyalvārkkut tuṇaiceyyum eṉṟu cuṭṭikkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
peṟutaṟkariya tammuṉṉōriṉ tarumap paṭṭayaṅkaḷai yāvarum aṟiyum vaṇṇam accu nūlvaṭivākki utaviya tiru.ku.paḻa.cu.paḻa.cuppiramaṇiya ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷukkut tamiḻulakam mikka kaṭappāṭu uṭaiyatu. ellā nakarattār kaḷuṭaiya poruḷutaviyiṉālum ivveḷiyīṭu varukiṉṟatu. nakarattārkaḷukku ellāt tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ naṉṟiyuriyatu.
ṭākṭar. va.cupa.māṇikkam
attāṭci urai
paḻanimalai vēlāyuta cuvāmiyārukku naṭantu varukiṟa tarma aṭṭavaṇai paṟṟiya “nakarattār aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷ” ēḻu aṭaṅkiya nūl oṉṟu neṟkuppai tiru. paḻa.cu. paḻa. cuppiramaṇiyaṉ ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷāl 22-7-61il muṉṉuraiyil aṟappaṭṭayaṅkaḷiṉ kaiyeḻuttup pirati oṉṟum ataṟkuc cariyāka acciṭap peṟṟa accup pirati oṉṟum eṉṉiṭam kāṭṭap peṟṟatāka appōtu eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.
mēṟkaṇṭa kaiyeḻuttu -accup piratikaḷukku mūlamāṉa eṭṭup piratikaḷai ippoḻutu paḻaṉi paṇṭārattaiyā vīṭṭiliruntu tēṭi eṭuttu vāṅkik koṇṭu vantu cupa. avarkaḷ kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. cuvaṭikaḷ paḻamai yāṉavaiyākavum, ki.pi. 1608il eḻutappeṟṟa cuvaṭiyil okkūruṭaiyāṉ uṭaiyappa ceṭṭi aṭaikkappaṉ eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1766il eḻutap peṟṟa cuvaṭiyil iḷanalamuṭaiyāṉ aṭaikkappa ceṭṭi aruṇācalam eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1788il eḻutappeṟṟa cuvaṭiyil piḷḷaiyār paṭṭik kōyil aruṇācalañ ceṭṭi kumarappaṉ eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum 1805il eḻutap peṟṟa cuvaṭiyil iluppaikkuṭi leṭcumaṇañ ceṭṭi aruṇācalam eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttum uṭaiyaṉavākavum kaiyeḻuttup piratiyil eḻutiyavarkaḷ kavaṉakkuṟaivāl viṭṭuviṭṭa cila coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷaik kāṭṭuvaṉavākavu mirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷaik kāṭṭuvaṉavākavumirukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṭṭup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷ accup piratiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷ - coṟṟoṭarkaḷōṭu poruḷ māṟupāṭuṭaiyaṉavākap perumpālumillai eṉṟālum ēṭṭup pirati kiṭaittataṉāl muṉṉar teḷivu peṟātirunta ciṟcila karuttukkaḷ ippōtu teḷivu peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. eṭuttukāṭṭukku accup piratiyiṉ 3-ām pakkattu 16,17 varikaḷil “mutal oru paṅkum iraṇṭu paṅkum uṇṭāccutu” eṉṟiruppatu ēṭṭup piratiyil “mutal orupaṅkum lāpam iraṇṭu paṅkum uṇṭāccutu” eṉṟu kāṇappeṟuvatai niṉaivūṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ.
immūla ātaravaik koṇṭu ivaikaḷiṟ kaṇṭa naṭaimuṟaikaḷ toṭakka kālantoṭṭu iṭaiyil evvita māṟṟamumiṉṟi nikaḻntu vantavai eṉpatai uṟutiyāka aṟintu koḷḷalām. putitākak kiṭaitta paḻaiya ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷil uḷḷapaṭi paṭṭayaṅkaḷai, aṭutta patippu veḷiyiṭum pōtu acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttuvārkaḷ.
maṟaintirunta mūlap piratiyai veḷippaṭuttit tanta srī ñāṉateṇṭāyutap perumāṉ nāṭṭuk kōṭṭai nakarattārkaḷukkum avarkaḷiṉ piratinitikaḷākap paḻani aṉṉatāṉa maṭattiṉ aṟappaṇikaḷai mēṟkoṇṭu toṇṭupurintu varum neṟkuppai ku.paḻa. kuṭumpattārukkum, kaṇṭaṉūr ku.pa.cu. kuṭumpattārukkum kāraikkuṭi kuma.peri.cu. paḻa. kuṭumpattārukkum paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭa naṭai muṟaikaḷiṉpaṭi peṟum aruṭ piracātaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilāka ellā nalaṉkaḷaiyum vaḻaṅkiyaruḷuvārāka!
nalam perukuka.
tēvakōṭṭai	vē. irāmanātaṉ,
5-3-363	pālakavi.


ku. nākappa paṇṭāram
